## llama-bench / one-off measurements

- **`bonsai_scalar_kernel`** (Ternary-Bonsai-2-27B-Abliterated-PQ2_0.gguf, commit bac645e, `-ngl 24 -fa 1 -t 6 --load-mode none`, /ai/src/llama.cpp/build (arch 75, pre-AVX2)): `{"results": {"pp1": 0.69, "pp2": 0.7, "pp3": 0.75, "pp4": 0.76, "pp8": 0.78, "tg24": 0.71}}` — PQ2_0 x Q8_0 vec_dot ran scalar (no VNNI); CPU-only tg 0.47
- **`bonsai_avx2_kernel`** (Ternary-Bonsai-2-27B-Abliterated-PQ2_0.gguf, commit b06d088, `-ngl 24 -fa 1 -t 6 --load-mode none`, /ai/src/llama.cpp/build (arch 75)): `{"results": {"tg": 2.99, "tg_cpu_only": 1.97, "tg_t12": "no better than -t 6"}}` — AVX2 kernel 38.5 -> 5.6 cycles/32 weights; perf: vec_dot 56%, libgomp spin 20%, mul 6%; DRAM ~13-15 of 38 GB/s
- **`bonsai_streamed_verify_arch75`** (Ternary-Bonsai-2-27B-Abliterated-PQ2_0.gguf, commit b06d088, `-ngl 24 -fa 1 -t 6 -p 1,2,4,8,16,32 -n 0`, arch 75, no FORCE_MMQ): `{"results_ms_per_pass": {"default_offload32": {"1": 334, "2": 676, "4": 1036, "8": 1743, "16": 3313, "32": 1354}, "offload2": {"1": 334, "2": 519, "4": 558, "8": 649, "16": 946, "32": 1364}}}` — GGML_OP_OFFLOAD_MIN_BATCH=2 streams CPU-resident weights over PCIe; verifying 8 tokens costs 1.94x one token
- **`bonsai_streamed_verify_builds`** (Ternary-Bonsai-2-27B-Abliterated-PQ2_0.gguf, commit 290857f, `GGML_OP_OFFLOAD_MIN_BATCH=2 -ngl 24 -fa 1 -t 6 -p 2,4,8,16 -n 0 -r 2`, build (61-virtual+MMQ+LTO) vs build75 (75+MMQ)): `{"results_ms_per_pass": {"build_61virtual": {"2": 519, "4": 558, "8": 650, "16": 694}, "build75": {"2": 531, "4": 570, "8": 659, "16": 955}}}` — batch-16 win comes from the Pascal code path; LTO: no effect (CPU-only 2.02 vs 1.97 tok/s)
- **`ptq1_vs_pq2_streamed`** (PQ2_0 vs PTQ1_0 (abliterated), commit 290857f, `GGML_OP_OFFLOAD_MIN_BATCH=2 -ngl 24`, /ai/src/llama.cpp/build75): `{"results_ms_per_pass": {"PQ2_0": {"2": 518, "4": 559, "8": 649, "tg_tps": 2.94}, "PTQ1_0": {"2": 539, "4": 694, "8": 815, "tg_tps": 0.58}}}` — PTQ1_0 slower despite 28% fewer bytes: GPU-side unpack compute-bound, CPU kernel scalar. Graft dropped.
- **`moe_thread_sweep`** (Qwen3.6 IQ2_M / Gemma4 IQ3_M, commit 290857f, `-ngl 99 -ot "exps=CPU" -fa 1 -p 0 -n 64 -r 2`, /ai/src/llama.cpp/build75): `{"results_tps": {"qwen36": {"t2": 14.15, "t3": 18.93, "t4": 22.4, "t5": 25.2, "t6": 26.05, "t6_no_cuda_graphs": 26.63}, "gemma4": {"t3": 11.06, "t4": 13.66, "t5": 15.4, "t6": 16.72, "t6_no_cuda_graphs": 16.95}}}` — fit T=a+b/t: Qwen 18.5 ms fixed + ~20 ms CPU experts; Gemma 21.7 + ~38. perf: iq2_s vec_dot 49% / iq3_s 49% + iq4_nl 12%; gomp spin 27-35%
